similarity search using\nconcurrent.futures for multi-core acceleration.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import cpu_count\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom sequential import (\n    Document,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult(IndexingResult):\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building (compatible with IndexingResult).\"\"\"\n    pass\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk_data: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents and return their term frequencies and vocabulary.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (chunk_id, list of documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n    \"\"\"\n    chunk_id, docs = chunk_data\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in docs:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return {\n        \"chunk_id\": chunk_id,\n        \"doc_term_freqs\": doc_term_freqs,\n        \"doc_terms\": doc_terms,\n        \"vocabulary\": vocabulary,\n    }\n\n\ndef _build_term_batch(batch_data: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index entries for a batch of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        batch_data: Tuple of (batch_id, list of terms, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict mapping terms to posting lists\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_id, terms, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = batch_data\n    local_index = {}\n\n    for term in terms:\n        idf_val = idf_dict[term]\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf_val\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        local_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    return {\"batch_id\": batch_id, \"index\": local_index}\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_batch(batch_data: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and compute norms for a batch of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        batch_data: Tuple of (batch_id, list of (doc_id, tf_dict), idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with doc_vectors and doc_norms\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_id, doc_items, idf_dict = batch_data\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_items:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            if term in idf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        norm = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    return {\"batch_id\": batch_id, \"doc_vectors\": doc_vectors, \"doc_norms\": doc_norms}\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: Optional[int] = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Parallelization strategy:\n    1. Parallel document tokenization and TF computation (chunked)\n    2. Sequential document frequency calculation (efficient with set operations)\n    3. Sequential IDF computation (fast operation)\n    4. Parallel inverted index building (by term batches)\n    5. Parallel document vector construction (by document batches)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF (parallel by chunks)\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    # Create document chunks\n    document_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunk = documents[i : i + chunk_size]\n        document_chunks.append((i // chunk_size, chunk))\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = {executor.submit(_process_document_chunk, chunk): chunk for chunk in document_chunks}\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            result = future.result()\n            doc_term_freqs.update(result[\"doc_term_freqs\"])\n            doc_terms.update(result[\"doc_terms\"])\n            vocabulary.update(result[\"vocabulary\"])\n\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast with set operations)\n    document_frequencies = {}\n    for term in vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    index.document_frequencies = document_frequencies\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    idf = {}\n    for term, df in document_frequencies.items():\n        idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n    index.idf = idf\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index (parallel by term batches)\n    terms_list = sorted(list(vocabulary))\n    inverted_index = {}\n\n    # Create term batches\n    terms_per_batch = max(1, len(terms_list) // num_workers)\n    term_batches = [\n        (i, terms_list[i : i + terms_per_batch], doc_term_freqs, idf)\n        for i in range(0, len(terms_list), terms_per_batch)\n    ]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = [executor.submit(_build_term_batch, batch) for batch in term_batches]\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            result = future.result()\n            inverted_index.update(result[\"index\"])\n\n    index.inverted_index = inverted_index\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms (parallel by document batches)\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    # Create document batches\n    doc_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n    docs_per_batch = max(1, len(doc_items) // num_workers)\n    doc_batches = [\n        (i, doc_items[i : i + docs_per_batch], idf)\n        for i in range(0, len(doc_items), docs_per_batch)\n    ]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = [executor.submit(_build_doc_vectors_batch, batch) for batch in doc_batches]\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            result = future.result()\n            doc_vectors.update(result[\"doc_vectors\"])\n            doc_norms.update(result[\"doc_norms\"])\n\n    index.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_query_worker(args: tuple) -> list[tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search multiple queries and return results with original indices.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (batch of (query_index, query), idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, documents_dict, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (query_index, results) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    batch, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, documents_dict, top_k = args\n    all_results = []\n\n    for query_index, query in batch:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            all_results.append((query_index, []))\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            all_results.append((query_index, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result objects\n        results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=documents_dict.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n\n        all_results.append((query_index, results))\n\n    return all_results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Each query is processed independently by a worker.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare documents dict for title lookup\n    documents_dict = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Create query work items with original indices\n    query_items = [\n        (i, query, index.idf, index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, documents_dict, top_k)\n        for i, query in enumerate(queries)\n    ]\n\n    results = [None] * len(queries)\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = {\n            executor.submit(_search_query_worker, item): item[0]\n            for item in query_items\n        }\n\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            query_idx, result = future.result()\n            results[query_idx] = result\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of workers\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    search_start = time.perf_counter()\n    search_results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query], parallel_result.index, args.top_k, documents=documents\n    )\n    search_time = time.perf_counter() - search_start\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(search_results[0])} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(search_results[0], 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}.
